# Trust & Authenticity Audit: Content Verification Report

**Date**: 2026-10-26T10:48:54.176Z
**Audited Files**: 148 HTML, JSON, and JS files
**Total Candidates Scanned**: 2002

## Audit Policy & Directive

Per strict instructions from the site owner:
1. **Clear Fake Reviews & Star Ratings**: Remove only fabricated testimonials with person names and arbitrary star ratings.
2. **Factual Claims / Certifications / Partners / Statistics**: Do **NOT** remove unilaterally. Flag with `needs proof` and catalog for site owner business verification.

## Categorized Findings Summary

| Category | Flagged Count | Action Policy |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| Numeric Claims & Statistics | 422 | Needs owner proof / keep as placeholder |
| Licensing & Awards | 342 | Needs owner proof / keep as placeholder |
| Star Ratings & Platforms | 988 | Remove fabricated names/ratings; keep schema clean |
| Reviews & Testimonials | 60 | Remove fabricated names/ratings; keep schema clean |
| Guarantees & Sustainability | 7 | Needs owner proof / keep as placeholder |
| Payment Providers | 183 | Needs owner proof / keep as placeholder |
